2026-11-19T15:17:53.1133347Z [36;1m# (byte-identical copy under the updater's asset name) + BUILD-INFO.txt.[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.1133847Z [36;1m# Format matches the standard ``sha256sum`` output so users can[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.1134313Z [36;1m# verify with ``sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS.txt`` on any platform.[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.1134675Z [36;1m$hashes = @()[0m

2026-11-19T15:17:53.1139284Z [36;1m$sha2 = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $info.FullName).Hash.ToLower()[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.1139687Z [36;1m$hashes += "$sha2  $($info.Name)"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.1139966Z [36;1mWrite-Host "$sha2  $($info.Name)"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.1140315Z [36;1m$hashes | Out-File -Encoding ascii artifacts/SHA256SUMS.txt[0m

2026-11-19T15:17:53.7297073Z ##[group]Run # Re-read the checksum from SHA256SUMS.txt and verify it matches
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7297739Z [36;1m# Re-read the checksum from SHA256SUMS.txt and verify it matches[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7298218Z [36;1m# the actual ZIP file. This catches a corrupt ZIP or a typo in[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7298585Z [36;1m# the checksum file.[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7298831Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7299129Z [36;1m# The ZIP is always written as the FIRST line of SHA256SUMS.txt[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7299583Z [36;1m# (see the Generate step). Take that line and extract the 64-char[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7300028Z [36;1m# hex hash before the first space. Avoid regex on the filename[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7300487Z [36;1m# (the `.` in PrintEXE-vX.Y.Z-win-x64.zip is a regex meta-char and[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7300893Z [36;1m# caused a mismatch in an earlier version).[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7301246Z [36;1m$lines = Get-Content artifacts/SHA256SUMS.txt[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7301745Z [36;1m$zipLine = $lines | Where-Object { $_ -like "*$env:ZIP_NAME*" } | Select-Object -First 1[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7302178Z [36;1mif (-not $zipLine) {[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7302515Z [36;1m  Write-Error "Could not find $env:ZIP_NAME in SHA256SUMS.txt"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7302867Z [36;1m  exit 1[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7303066Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7303331Z [36;1m# The hash is the first 64 chars (SHA-256 hex). Use a literal[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7303969Z [36;1m# substring split on the first space, not a regex split.[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7304314Z [36;1m$idx = $zipLine.IndexOf(' ')[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7304584Z [36;1mif ($idx -lt 64) {[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7304874Z [36;1m  Write-Error "Malformed checksum line: $zipLine"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7305172Z [36;1m  exit 1[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7305355Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7305593Z [36;1m$expected = $zipLine.Substring(0, 64).ToLower()[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7306029Z [36;1m$actual = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $env:ZIP_PATH).Hash.ToLower()[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7306445Z [36;1mWrite-Host "Expected: $expected"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7306727Z [36;1mWrite-Host "Actual:   $actual"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7307002Z [36;1mif ($expected -ne $actual) {[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7307381Z [36;1m  Write-Error "Checksum mismatch! Expected: $expected  Actual: $actual"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7307756Z [36;1m  exit 1[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7307948Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7308250Z [36;1m# Phase 6: the portable updater asset must be BYTE-IDENTICAL to the[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7308740Z [36;1m# versioned ZIP — it is the same file under the updater's asset name.[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7309287Z [36;1m$portableHash = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $env:PORTABLE_PATH).Hash.ToLower()[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7309742Z [36;1mif ($portableHash -ne $actual) {[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7310501Z [36;1m  Write-Error "PrintShopCompanion-portable.zip is not byte-identical to $env:ZIP_NAME"[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7311024Z [36;1m  exit 1[0m
2026-11-19T15:17:53.7311564Z [36;1m}[0m
